  1. Simulation outputs represent 2025, averaged over a 4-year calibration window (2022–2025). Values are medians (5th–95th percentile) across 3 accepted calibration runs.
  2. Only organism–drug combinations where the drug has non-negligible potency (baseline potency >= 0.15) are shown.
  3. Percent of infections with resistance: percentage of active infections carrying any resistance to this drug at a point in time (simulated vs. surveillance estimate).
  4. Average resistance level among resistant infection-days: among infection-days where any resistance is present, the mean resistance level expressed as a percentage (0–100%). A value near 100% indicates that resistance, when present, is essentially complete; lower values indicate partial resistance. This is distinct from the prevalence column above, which measures the proportion of infection-days with any resistance.
  5. Percent of people carrying the bacterium in whom a resistant strain is present: percentage of the global population carrying a resistant strain of this organism in the gut or upper respiratory microbiome.
